## `ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ARM` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Refractoriness-Under-Load Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Refractory Refractoriness-under-Load & Cold Crushing Strength
- **Intro:** Refractoriness-Under-Load Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Refractory Refractoriness-under-Load & Cold Crushing Strength — Pre-Operational Verification and System Readiness: Hydraulic pressure: 200±2 bar. Nitrogen pressure: 0.5±0.05 bar. Furnace ambient temperature: 25±5°C. Relative humidity: ≤60%.
- **Key tolerance:** Hydraulic pressure: 200±2 bar. Nitrogen pressure: 0.5±0.05 bar. Furnace ambient temperature: 25±5°C. Relative humidity: ≤60%.
- **Standards:** `ISO 1893:2007`, `ISO 10059-1:1992`, `ASTM C16-03(2018) Standard Test Method for Refractoriness of Ref`, `ASTM C133-97(2015) Standard Test Methods for Cold Crushing Stren`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/ARM-2391-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
